The manifests specify thirty optimizer steps. P11 therefore does not
satisfy the report's Tier A threshold of at least one hundred steps,
despite its eight-seed design. The reported contrast, confidence
interval, power estimate and null result remain unchanged. Failed and
superseded attempts are not pooled with the accepted forty units.

\section{Recovered campaign evidence}\label{recovered-campaign-evidence}

Six unchanged original files resolved five named author-side recovery
gaps; their absence from the public package remains a reproducibility
limitation. Their private filesystem and export metadata are not
included here. The scientific availability result is narrower than
validation of every claim: LAB-Bench records 450 correct of 1,967;
AgentDojo records 88 utility passes across 97 benign episodes, with
security evaluation false and no held-out claim. The recovered
training-identity note explicitly disclaims content decontamination and
does not provide the dirty source diff.

Bounded stored-payload checks cover 29 explicit file-reference records
across 27 targets, 1,979 E8 archive/local bindings and 525 E10
archive-member bindings. The selected 1,967 E8 grades and 97 E10 traces
reproduce the stored success counts of 450 and 88. E8's 1,259
unparsed-answer total remains diagnostic-supported rather than
independently recalculated from the reduced grade rows. These are
structural, hash and tally checks, not model execution, scorer reruns or
independent semantic grading.

For E14, the native judged-row accuracy is 2,271/4,426 (51.31\%);
counting the two unjudged rows as failures gives the conservative
all-disposition diagnostic 2,271/4,428 (51.29\%). Section 8.2 explains
the denominator-label erratum; no source bytes or judgments are changed.
The omitted full payload still prevents a fresh public-package recount
or independent semantic grading.

The recovered finish-era chronology says the E4 full rerun had not
launched at its September 21 snapshot, while the later secondary summary
dates the claimed execution to that day. Their ordering is not
established. With no primary job receipt or exact launch timestamp, the
full-rerun outcome and trajectory counts are excluded as empirical
evidence. Original training-log payloads, the dirty source diff and
content-level decontamination also remain unresolved.

\section{Preserved C1 result and residual
limits}\label{preserved-c1-result-and-residual-limits}

The C1 parser-defined endpoint remains 11/64. The conservative review
supports three numerical recovery witnesses on the same denominator;
these counts are separate and neither establishes training improvement.
The sixty-four reference classifications, eleven event records,
scientific hashes and disagreements remain in the included case ledger.
Historical V2 findings remain separate and unpooled.

This package excludes private provider exports, authenticated inventory
and reconciliation ledgers, raw access/budget chronologies, private
notebook links, original recovery-export wrappers, personal signature
data and account identifiers. Omission is a publication boundary, not
evidence that a scientific result failed. Conversely, a retained source
reference or checksum is not proof that the cited bytes are publicly
available or that all claims have been independently verified. The
public PDF has a distinct title and filename and does not replace the
preserved historical manuscript or certify institutional approval.



\chapter{Historical Number-Audit
Snapshot}\label{historical-number-audit-snapshot}

\textbf{Revision boundary (18 October 2026).} The registry below is
retained as an earlier audit snapshot. Its locations, occurrence counts
and PASS statuses refer to the pre-revision manuscript; they have not
been regenerated or certified against this revised thesis. Several
formerly registered claims are now withdrawn. The new source package
supplies a fresh file-integrity manifest and a revision verification
report, which establish file identity and document checks, not
experimental authenticity. Historical table labels below intentionally
retain their original names.

This historical registry covers selected numeric token occurrences, not
every number or scientific claim in the thesis. This table and
\texttt{provenance\_audit.json} are outputs of the earlier audit; the
JSON records each formerly registered token, its check and cited-file
hashes. Do not overwrite these historical files when checking the
revision. The audit tool writes outputs even with \texttt{-\/-check};
direct any new diagnostic to separate \texttt{-\/-json} and
\texttt{-\/-appendix} paths and review its claim registry before
interpreting results. A PASS means the token appears verbatim in its
chapter and satisfies its named check; it does not establish independent
scientific verification. Methods: R recomputed from rows (counts, means,
SDs, Wilson, Fisher, t, TOST, ANOVA, McNemar, sign-flip, cluster
bootstrap, \texttt{git\ apply\ -\/-stat}); S stored summary field; C
hyperparameter matched in the runner code; T numeric transcription in
the cited source report; P selected cited-prose tokens matched to a
source or another registered check; A arithmetic from printed counts,
without independently checking those inputs against an artifact; W
withheld source, not checked. CONTEXT includes definitions and
unverified printed inputs; these tokens are not independently checked
and are excluded from passing-check counts. Σ marks the SHA-256 of a
group's sorted \texttt{sha256\ \ path} lines. Hashes bind the author's
checkout; Appendix I states which files the public package includes.

Earlier number-to-artifact registry for Chapters 1, 4 and 6--9 and
Appendices A and F. In that earlier snapshot, of 969 registered token
occurrences, 947 checks pass and 22 are context only, not independently
checked. The passing checks include 34 calculations from printed counts
only. 0 occurrences rest on withheld sources and are not checked; 0
checks fail, 0 are unbound and 0 lack required artifacts. Passing this
registry does not verify every numerical thesis claim or authenticate
its sources.
